Every approach to tabular AI today, from gradient-boosted trees to the latest tabular foundation models, requires a preprocessing pipeline before any model can consume the data. None of them understand tabular data as a modality. We introduce the Data Language Model (DLM), the missing foundation model for tabular data. A DLM understands tables the way a language model understands sentences: natively, without serialization or preprocessing, directly from raw cell values. It is the tabular data layer on which AI models, agents, and vertical AI applications can be built, eliminating the preprocessing pipelines that currently stand between raw data and every AI system that consumes it. We present Schema-1, the first DLM: a 140M parameter model trained on more than 2.3M synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. Schema-1 outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and the tabular foundation models we evaluate on established row-level prediction benchmarks. On missing value reconstruction it achieves lower reconstruction error than all classical statistical methods and frontier large language models on mean performance across conditions, establishing that structural understanding of a dataset's own distributional geometry is more useful for imputation than world knowledge encoded in language. It identifies the industry sector of any unseen dataset from raw cell values alone, reliably across any domain, a task no prior tabular model can perform. It is the native tabular understanding layer that has been missing from the AI stack.

</details>


### [146] [Addressing Labelled Data Scarcity: Taxonomy-Agnostic Annotation of PII Values in HTTP Traffic using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06305)
*Thomas Cory,Axel Küpper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的多阶段流水线，能够根据运行时提供的分类法，对HTTP消息体中显式传输的个人身份信息（PII）进行与分类法无关的自动标注。通过结合确定性预处理、标签级分类、实例级值标注和输出验证，并在合成HTTP流量上评估，结果表明该方法能准确检测PII类型并提取对应值，为灵活、可迁移的隐私审计流量标注提供了新基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的PII检测器依赖稀缺的手工标注流量，且与固定标签分类法紧密耦合，限制了跨领域迁移性和对PII定义演变的适应性。作者希望探索大语言模型能否支持分类法无关的、运行时给定分类法的显式PII值标注。

Method: 提出一个多阶段LLM流水线，包含确定性预处理、标签级分类、目标实例级值标注和输出验证四个阶段。同时，为规避真实用户数据隐私风险，设计了基于LLM的合成HTTP流量生成器，其PII标注由人工验证。使用三种不同粒度和领域的PII分类法进行评估。

Result: 流水线在具体PII分类法下能够准确检测PII类型并提取对应的值。合成流量生成方法能够提供可控的评估数据。

Conclusion: LLM可作为灵活、分类法无关的流量标注的有前景的基础，并可用于在隐私分类法不断演变的情况下创建标注数据。

Abstract: Automated privacy audits of web and mobile applications often analyse outbound HTTP traffic to detect Personally Identifiable Information (PII) leakage. However, existing learning-based detectors typically depend on scarce, manually labelled traffic and are tightly coupled to fixed label taxonomies, limiting transferability across domains and evolving definitions of PII. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can support taxonomy-agnostic annotation of explicitly transmitted PII values in HTTP message bodies when the taxonomy is provided at runtime. We introduce a multi-stage LLM-based pipeline that combines deterministic pre-processing with label-level classification, targeted instance-level value annotation, and output validation. To enable controlled evaluation and exemplar-based prompting without relying on sensitive real-user captures, we further propose an LLM-based generator for synthetic HTTP traffic with manually validated, taxonomy-derived PII annotations. We evaluate the approach across three taxonomies spanning different PII domains and granularity levels. Results show that the pipeline accurately detects PII types and extracts corresponding values for concrete PII taxonomies. Overall, our findings position LLMs as a promising foundation for flexible, taxonomy-agnostic traffic annotation and for creating labelled data under evolving privacy taxonomies.

</details>


### [147] [More Than Can Be Said: A Benchmark and Framework for Pre-Question Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06345)
*Jie Yu,Song Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架InciteResearch，将研究者模糊的隐性认知转化为结构化显性研究问题，并引入首个隐性到显性研究基准TF-Bench，效果显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究助手假定研究者已拥有清晰可操作的研究问题，而人类研究往往始于模糊的前语言摩擦（tacit friction），即问题尚未形成时的错位感。缺乏能将隐性认知显性化的系统。

Method: InciteResearch框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 从模糊甚至无关输入中提取结构化五维研究者状态（锚定摩擦点）；(2) 通过最大化可行性-新颖性乘积并强制执行7阶段因果推导轨迹来违背隐藏假设；(3) 验证提出方法是否为重构洞察的必要推论。同时构建TF-Bench基准，区分领域相关与无关灵感。

Result: 在TF-Bench上，InciteResearch相比提示基线取得跨越式提升（新颖性从3.671提升至4.250，影响力从3.806提升至4.397），生成提案从重组型转向架构洞察型。

Conclusion: AI可以成为思维本身的延伸，而不仅仅是自动化下游执行工具。

Abstract: AI research agents have shown strong potential in automating literature search and manuscript refinement, yet most assume a clear and actionable initial input, operating only after a research question has been made explicit. In contrast, human research often begins with tacit friction, a sense of misalignment before a question can be formed. We introduce InciteResearch, a multi-agent framework designed to make a researcher's implicit understanding explicit, inspectable, and actionable. InciteResearch decomposes the logical chain of Socratic questioning and distributes it across the entire pipeline that: (1) Elicits a structured five-dimensional researcher profile state anchored by specific friction points from vague, even domain-unrelated inputs; (2) Violates hidden assumptions by maximizing the feasibility-novelty product with enforcing a 7-stage causal derivation trace; and (3) check whether the proposed method is a Necessary consequence of the reframed insight. We further introduce TF-Bench, the first benchmark for tacit-to-explicit research assistance that distinguishes domain-related from domain-unrelated inspirations across four scientific modes. On TF-Bench, InciteResearch achieves leapfrogging gains over a prompt-based baseline (novelty/impact from 3.671/3.806 to 4.250/4.397), shifting generated proposals from recombination to architectural insight. Our work demonstrates that AI can serve as an extension of thinking itself, rather than merely automating downstream execution.

</details>


### [148] [Prediction and Empowerment: A Theory of Agency through Bridge Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06346)
*Richard Csaky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在确定性物理/模拟世界中研究部分可观测下的智能体机制，证明预测、压缩与赋权之间存在分离：完美预测可通过识别隐藏商或覆盖控制实现，而高赋权不足以保证预测。在可精炼接口和充足记忆条件下，动作条件化的观测压缩能减少潜在商的后验不确定性，并产生目标条件化的接口赋权。比特串特例中的信息预算守恒使权衡显式化：识别预测需内部容量≥相关潜在熵，覆盖控制需终端动作容量≥受控商。对现代AI，建议区分隐藏态识别、接口精炼、任务可控性与覆盖/干扰控制，人机对齐本质是接口设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在确定性世界中（物理或模拟）部分可观测条件下智能体的预测、压缩与赋权之间的本质关系，澄清高赋权是否足以保证完美预测，并探讨不同预测策略（识别与覆盖控制）的信息需求差异。

Method: 将传感与执行建模为桥接接口，分为智能体控制参数和环境控制信道状态；通过潜在微状态先验和多对一观测粗粒化诱导确定性POMDP；理论证明预测、压缩与赋权的分离；通过比特串特例化使信息预算守恒下的权衡显式化。

Result: 完美预测可通过两种方式实现：识别隐藏商（需内部容量≥相关潜在熵）或覆盖控制使未来目标动作决定（需终端动作容量≥受控商）。高赋权单独不足以保证预测。在可精炼接口和充足记忆下，动作条件化观测压缩减少潜在商后验不确定性，精炼需引导世界侧信道条件时产生目标条件化接口赋权。

Conclusion: 目标函数应区分隐藏态识别、接口精炼、任务可控性与覆盖/干扰控制；人机对齐本质是接口设计问题，桥接人类意图、智能体内部状态、外部工具与世界侧信道条件。

Abstract: We study agency under partial observability in deterministic physical or simulated worlds, where apparent randomness arises from uncertainty over initial conditions, fixed law bits, and unrolled exogenous noise. We model sensing and actuation as bridge interfaces split between agent-controlled parameters and environment-controlled channel state, inducing a deterministic POMDP through a prior over latent microstates and many-to-one observation coarsening. Within this framework, we prove a separation between prediction, compression, and empowerment. Perfect prediction can be achieved either by identifying the hidden quotient relevant to the target family or by overwrite control that makes the future target action-determined; high empowerment alone is insufficient. Under refinable interfaces and sufficient memory, action-conditioned observation-compression progress reduces posterior uncertainty about the latent quotient, and when refinement requires steering world-side channel conditions, this creates target-conditioned interface empowerment. A bit-string specialization with a conserved information budget makes the resulting tradeoff explicit: prediction by identification requires internal capacity at least the relevant latent entropy, whereas overwrite control requires terminal action capacity over the controlled quotient. For modern AI agents, the results suggest a design principle rather than a theorem of inevitability: objectives should distinguish hidden-state identification, interface refinement, task-relevant controllability, and mere overwrite or distractor control. Human--AI alignment is partly an interface-design problem, where the relevant bridge is between human intent, agent internal state, external tools, and world-side channel conditions. This is a working draft: feedback and criticism is most welcome.

</details>


### [149] [From Agent Loops to Deterministic Graphs: Execution Lineage for Reproducible AI-Native Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06365)
*Josh Rosen,Seth Rosen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**执行谱系（Execution Lineage）**模型，将AI原生工作表示为带显式依赖关系的有向无环图（DAG），通过稳定的中间边界和基于身份的重放机制，使演化中的AI生成内容在变更场景下保持可维护性。实验表明，相比循环式基线，DAG重放在无关分支更新中实现了零污染与零变动，在中间产物编辑中实现了完美上游保留、下游传播、未影响产物保留及跨产物一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型系统在agentic工作流（推理、工具使用、记忆、迭代优化）中依赖隐式对话状态，导致工作产物难以稳定保存、无关更新无法隔离、中间产物的变更传播困难。亟需一种显式的执行模型来管理AI生成工作的演化过程。

Method: 提出**执行谱系**模型：将AI原生工作建模为**有向无环图（DAG）**，其中节点为产生工件的计算，边为显式依赖关系；引入稳定的中间边界和基于身份的重放机制。在两项策略备忘录更新任务上与循环式基线对比：①无关分支更新；②中间产物编辑。

Result: ①无关分支更新：DAG重放零变动、零污染地保留最终备忘录，循环基线频繁引入无关上下文。②中间产物编辑：所有系统都能反映新约束，但仅DAG重放实现完美上游保留、下游传播、未影响产物保留及跨产物一致性。

Conclusion: 最终答案质量与维护状态质量是两种不同的维度。循环基线在单轮合成/更新任务中可产出高质量的最终输出，但即时的任务成功可能掩盖部分状态不一致，这种不一致会在多次修订中累积。执行谱系为"什么应该变更、什么应该保持稳定、工作如何跨版本演化"提供了更强的保证。

Abstract: Large language model systems are increasingly deployed as agentic workflows that interleave reasoning, tool use, memory, and iterative refinement. These systems are effective at producing answers, but they often rely on implicit conversational state, making it difficult to preserve stable work products, isolate irrelevant updates, or propagate changes through intermediate artifacts.
  We introduce execution lineage: an execution model in which AI-native work is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of artifact-producing computations with explicit dependencies, stable intermediate boundaries, and identity-based replay. The goal is not to make the model a better one-shot writer, but to make evolving AI-generated work maintainable under change.
  We compare execution-lineage replay against loop-centric update baselines on two controlled policy-memo update tasks. In an unrelated-branch update, DAG replay preserved the final memo exactly in all runs, with zero churn and zero unrelated-branch contamination, while loop baselines regenerated the memo and frequently imported unrelated context. In an intermediate-artifact edit, all systems reflected the new constraint in the final memo, but only DAG replay achieved perfect upstream preservation, downstream propagation, unaffected-artifact preservation, and cross-artifact consistency.
  These results show that final answer quality and maintained-state quality are distinct. Strong loop baselines can remain competitive at producing polished final outputs when the task is a bounded synthesis/update problem and all current sources fit in context, but immediate task success can mask partial state inconsistency that may compound over future revisions. Execution lineage provides stronger guarantees about what should change, what should remain stable, and how work evolves across revisions.

</details>


### [150] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明，基于空性（不确定性质量）的OOD检测方法在证据深度学习中对ID和OOD类别基数差异高度敏感，即使仅相差1个类别，AUROC和AUPR也会显著偏差（如AUROC偏差高达0.360），且这种偏差无需模型预测变化即可人为膨胀评估指标。文章还讨论了大语言模型在多项选择问答场景下的OOD定义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示EDL中空性（不确定性质量）作为OOD检测指标时，因ID和OOD类别基数（K）不等而导致的评估伪影问题，指出该指标在类别基数不一致时会产生误导性的性能评估结果。

Method: 通过理论分析和实证实验，比较标准EDL和IB-EDL在ID与OOD类别基数相差1时AUROC和AUPR指标的变化，并讨论因果语言模型在MCQA数据集上的OOD评估定义问题。

Result: 当ID和OOD类别基数仅差1时，标准EDL的AUROC差异高达0.318，AUPR差异高达0.613；IB-EDL的AUROC差异高达0.360，AUPR差异高达0.683。这种偏差是人为评估伪影，可在模型预测不变的情况下人为膨胀指标。

Conclusion: 基于空性的OOD检测在EDL微调的大语言模型中高度依赖于ID和OOD的类别基数差异，必须确保K_ID和K_OOD相等才能进行有效比较；同时需要在MCQA等场景下对ID和OOD给出更清晰的定义。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [151] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于验证知识图谱（KG）的多智能体工作流，通过从规范、RTL和形式工具反馈中提取结构化中间表示（IR），生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA），并利用语法修复、反例引导和覆盖率驱动的三重优化循环提升断言质量，在七个基准设计中实现78.5%-99.4%的形式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成SVA的方法将规范和RTL视为松散文本，导致规范到RTL的接地薄弱，产生语义不匹配和频繁的语法失败。为解决这些问题，需要一种能够将需求、设计层次、信号、假设和属性链接起来的结构化上下文。

Method: 构建验证中心知识图谱，从规范、RTL和形式工具反馈（语法诊断、反例、覆盖报告）中提取结构化中间表示（IR）。采用多智能体工作流，通过查询和更新KG生成SVA，并驱动三个优化循环：语法修复（工具诊断引导）、反例纠正（迹线链接引导）、覆盖驱动属性增强。

Result: 在七个基准设计上的评估表明：基于KG的上下文检索改善了规范到RTL的接地，持续生成可编译的SVA且语法修复开销低，形式覆盖率达78.5%-99.4%。但收敛性存在设计依赖性，复杂时序和算术推理对当前LLM仍有挑战。

Conclusion: 知识图谱方法有效增强了SVA生成的规范-RTL接地和语法正确性，但复杂时序与算术推理场景下LLM能力仍存在局限，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [152] [SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)
*Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Skyler Wang,Brandon Handoko,Antonio Li,Anaelia Ovalle,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Candace Ross,Vidya Sarma,Arjun Subramonian,Karen Ullrich,Will van der Vaart,Yijing Xin,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCRuB框架，用于评估大语言模型在社会概念推理方面的能力。该框架通过构建提示、生成专家与模型回答、使用五维批判性思维评分标准进行比较评估。实验发现，前沿模型在所有五个维度上均超越人类专家，在80.8%的判断中被专家评为第一，74.4%的情况下专家更偏好模型回答。研究表明，单轮考试式评估已触及天花板，模型和人类均达到饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理能力研究多聚焦于数学或技术任务，鲜有涉及社会概念（塑造社会规范、文化和制度的抽象思想）的推理评估。随着LLM作为社会智能体的应用日益广泛，评估其社会概念推理能力变得至关重要，但缺乏系统化的评估方法。

Method: SCRuB框架包含三个阶段：(1) 从权威来源构建提示；(2) 由专家和模型分别生成回答；(3) 使用五维批判性思维评分标准进行比较评估。引入学科视角专家组（Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives）集成方法，并通过独立专家评委进行验证。发布了SCRuBEval（4,711个评估提示）和SCRuBAnnotations（300个专家回答和150个专家比较判断，来自45位博士级学者）。

Result: 前沿模型在所有五个评分维度上均一致超越人类专家。在1,170次成对比较中，专家评委在80.8%的判断中将模型回答排在首位，74.4%的情况下专家更偏好模型回答。研究提供了首个基于专家的社会概念推理评估饱和证明：单轮考试式评估格式已到达模型和人类的天花板。

Conclusion: 研究表明，当前最先进的LLM在社会概念推理方面已超越人类专家水平，且现有的单轮考试式评估范式已无法进一步区分模型和人类的表现差异。这提示需要开发更复杂的评估方法来推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: While many studies of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities emphasize mathematical or technical tasks, few address reasoning about social concepts: the abstract ideas shaping social norms, culture, and institutions. This understudied capability is essential for modern models acting as social agents, yet no systematic evaluation methodology targets it. We introduce SCRuB (Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation), a framework designed for this setting of task indeterminacy. Our goal is to measure the degree to which a model reasons about social concepts with the depth and critical rigor of a human expert. SCRuB proceeds in three phases: prompt construction from established sources, response generation by experts and models, and comparative evaluation using a five-dimensional critical thinking rubric. To enable generalization of the pipeline, we introduce a Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives ensemble validated against independent expert judges. We release SCRuBEval (n=4,711 evaluation prompts) and SCRuBAnnotations (300 expert-authored responses and 150 expert comparative judgments from 45 PhD-level scholars). Our results show that frontier models consistently outperform human experts across all five rubric dimensions. Across 1,170 pairwise comparisons, expert judges ranked a model response first in 80.8% of judgments and preferred model responses overall 74.4% of the time. Ultimately, this study provides the first expert-grounded demonstration of evaluation saturation for social concept reasoning: the single-turn exam-style format has reached its ceiling for models and humans alike.

</details>


### [153] [PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455)
*Xinmiao Huang,Jinwei Hu,Rajarshi Roy,Changshun Wu,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrefixGuard是一个将原始执行轨迹转化为在线前缀监控器的框架，通过离线StepView归纳步骤和监督学习训练，在多个基准测试上显著优于基于原始文本的监控和LLM直接评判方法，并提供了可观测性上限和有穷自动机提取等诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行长序列工具调用任务时，最终结果检查往往到来过晚，无法及时干预。在线预警需要轻量级的前缀监控器来处理异构轨迹，但手工编写事件模式脆弱且部署时调用LLM作为评判器成本过高。

Method: 提出PrefixGuard框架：1）离线StepView归纳步骤，从原始轨迹样本中诱导出确定性的类型化步骤适配器；2）监督训练监控器，学习事件抽象和前缀风险评分器，以终端结果为监督信号。

Result: 在WebArena、τ²-Bench、SkillsBench和TerminalBench上，最强PrefixGuard监控器分别达到0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC，比原始文本基线平均提升+0.137 AUPRC，显著优于LLM评判器。导出可观测性上限区分监控器误差与缺乏证据的情况。有穷自动机在WebArena和τ²-Bench上紧凑（29和20状态），在SkillsBench和TerminalBench上扩展至151和187状态。预警诊断显示WebArena排名好但难以支持低误报预警，τ²-Bench和TerminalBench保留更多可操作的早期预警。

Conclusion: PrefixGuard提供了一套实用的监控器合成方案，并附带明确的诊断方法来判断前缀预警能否转化为可操作的干预措施。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now execute long, tool-using tasks where final outcome checks can arrive too late for intervention. Online warning requires lightweight prefix monitors over heterogeneous traces, but hand-authored event schemas are brittle and deployment-time LLM judging is costly. We introduce PrefixGuard, a trace-to-monitor framework with an offline StepView induction step followed by supervised monitor training. StepView induces deterministic typed-step adapters from raw trace samples, and the monitor learns an event abstraction and prefix-risk scorer from terminal outcomes. Across WebArena, $τ^2$-Bench, SkillsBench, and TerminalBench, the strongest PrefixGuard monitors reach 0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC. Using the strongest backend within each representation, they improve over raw-text controls by an average of +0.137 AUPRC. LLM judges remain substantially weaker under the same prefix-warning protocol. We also derive an observability ceiling on score-based area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) that separates monitor error from failures lacking evidence in the observed prefix. For finite-state audit, post-hoc deterministic finite automaton (DFA) extraction remains compact on WebArena and $τ^2$-Bench (29 and 20 states) but expands to 151 and 187 states on SkillsBench and TerminalBench. Finally, first-alert diagnostics show that strong ranking does not imply deployment utility: WebArena ranks well yet fails to support low-false-alarm alerts, whereas $τ^2$-Bench and TerminalBench retain more actionable early alerts. Together, these results position PrefixGuard as a practical monitor-synthesis recipe with explicit diagnostics for when prefix warnings translate into actionable interventions.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06457)
*Donghao Huang,Joon Kiat Chua,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Success Rate (ASR)指标，用于评估LLM多智能体系统在支付工作流中的轨迹保真度。通过对18个LLM和90,000个任务实例的HMASP系统测试，发现半数模型系统性跳过确认检查点（传统指标无法检测），而基于ASR的诊断改进可将任务成功率提升高达93.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指标（TSR和HF1）仅捕获最终结果或无序路由决策，无法检测多智能体系统在执行工作流时的轨迹偏差，特别是在支付等受监管领域中，确认检查点等关键步骤的遗漏可能带来严重后果。

Method: 提出Agentic Success Rate (ASR)轨迹保真度指标，从转移级别比较观察到的和预期的智能体执行序列，分解为转移召回率（Transition Recall）和转移精确率（Transition Precision）。在Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP)系统上使用18个LLM和90,000个任务实例进行实验，并结合提示优化和确定性路由防护进行改进。

Result: 10/18的模型系统性地跳过支付结账确认检查点（TSR和HF1无法检测），8个模型完美执行检查点；GPT-4.1在TSR和HF1完美的情况下仍存在隐藏的工作流捷径；GPT-5.2实现完美ASR；基于ASR诊断的改进使之前表现不佳的模型TSR提升高达+93.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 轨迹级别评估对于受监管领域的多智能体系统至关重要，ASR指标能揭示传统指标忽略的执行偏差，显著提升系统可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for payment workflows, yet prevailing metrics, Task Success Rate (TSR) and Agent Handoff F1-Score (HF1), capture only final outcomes or unordered routing decisions. We introduce the Agentic Success Rate (ASR), a trajectory-fidelity metric that compares observed and expected agent execution sequences at the transition level, decomposing performance into Transition Recall and Transition Precision. Applied to the Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP) across 18 LLMs and 90,000 task instances, ASR reveals that 10 of 18 models systematically skip a confirmation checkpoint during payment checkout, a deviation invisible to both TSR and HF1, while 8 models enforce the checkpoint perfectly. Notably, GPT-4.1 exhibits hidden workflow shortcuts despite achieving perfect TSR and HF1, while GPT-5.2 achieves perfect ASR. Prompt refinements and deterministic routing guards guided by ASR diagnostics yield substantial TSR improvements, with gains up to +93.8 percentage points for previously struggling models, demonstrating that trajectory-level evaluation is essential in regulated domains.

</details>


### [155] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonSTL提出了一种工具增强框架，利用本地开源大语言模型将自然语言翻译成信号时序逻辑（STL）公式，通过推理分解、确定性工具调用和过程奖励训练，在保护隐私、降低成本的同时实现了最先进的翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言到STL的翻译面临三个主要挑战：手动编写需要时序逻辑专业知识且难以规模化；使用商业LLM API会产生大量token成本；将系统需求暴露给第三方服务存在隐私风险，不适合工业部署。

Method: ReasonSTL框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将翻译过程分解为显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建；2) 引入过程奖励训练（process-rewarded training）来监督工具使用轨迹和最终公式；3) 构建了STL-Bench双语计算感知基准数据集。框架基于本地开源语言模型运行。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，使用ReasonSTL训练的4B参数模型均达到了最先进的性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL为形式化规格草拟提供了一种透明、低成本且保护隐私的替代方案，使得在工业场景中部署自然语言到STL的翻译成为可能。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [156] [From Token Lists to Graph Motifs: Weisfeiler-Lehman Analysis of Sparse Autoencoder Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06494)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pablo Magariños-Docampo,Javier Perez-Robles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图结构的SAE特征表示方法，通过构建token共现图并设计图核函数进行特征聚类，发现了解码器权重和token直方图无法捕获的互补性结构关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征分析几乎完全依赖top-activating token列表或解码器权重向量，忽略了特征间的高阶共现结构信息。

Method: 将每个SAE特征建模为token共现图（节点为强激活附近高频token，边为局部窗口内共现关系），设计了一种自定义的WL风格频率分箱图核函数进行相似度度量，并在GPT-2 Small上使用合成多领域语料库进行聚类分析。

Result: 聚类恢复了标点模式、语言/脚本簇和类代码模板等启发式主题簇，这些是解码器余弦相似度聚类无法恢复的；token直方图基线在整体纯度上更优，图视图贡献是互补而非主导的。

Conclusion: 图结构视角能够揭示token频率和解码器权重视图无法捕获的结构关系，且聚类结果对超参数和随机种子具有稳定性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become central to mechanistic interpretability, decomposing transformer activations into monosemantic features. Yet existing analyses characterise features almost exclusively through top-activating token lists or decoder weight vectors, leaving the higher-order co-occurrence structure shared across features largely unexamined. We introduce a graph-structured representation in which each SAE feature is modelled as a token co-occurrence graph: nodes are the tokens most frequent near strong activations, and edges connect pairs that co-occur within local context windows. A custom WL-style, frequency-binned graph kernel then provides a similarity measure over this structural space. Applied as a proof of concept to features from a large SAE trained on GPT-2 Small and probed with a synthetic mixed-domain corpus, our clustering recovers heuristic motif families (punctuation-heavy patterns, language and script clusters, and code-like templates) that are not recovered by clustering on decoder cosine similarity. A token-histogram baseline achieves higher overall purity, so the contribution of the graph view is complementary rather than dominant: it surfaces structural relationships that token-frequency and decoder-weight views alone do not capture. Cluster assignments are stable across graph-construction hyperparameters and random seeds.

</details>


### [157] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpatialEpiBench是一个面向时空传染病预测的标准化基准，包含11个数据集、滚动评估和爆发专用指标。评估发现，大多数基于邻接关系的预测模型在1天至1个月的预测中甚至无法超越简单的"上次观测值"基线，主要失败模式包括爆发预测不足、处理稀疏噪声数据困难以及地理邻接信息效用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化的时空传染病预测基准，现有评估多采用简单的时序划分方式，无法反映真实预测场景。为填补这一空白，需要建立一个具有挑战性、贴近公共卫生实践的标准评估框架。

Method: 构建了包含11个传染病数据集的基准，采用标准化滚动评估方案和爆发专用评估指标。系统评估了基于邻接信息的预测模型，并结合广泛使用的传染病先验知识对通用模型进行适配。

Result: 大多数时空预测模型在1天至1个月的预测窗口内表现低于简单的"上次观测值"基线，即使在爆发场景和加入先验知识的情况下也是如此。识别出三个主要失败模式：爆发预测不足、对稀疏和噪声数据的处理能力差、地理邻接信息在流行病学中的空间信息效用有限。

Conclusion: 现有基于地理邻接的时空预测方法在传染病预测中存在根本性局限，需要开发更具操作实用性的预测模型。基准数据、代码和说明已公开以促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [158] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有AI创意系统评估忽视了一个关键盲点：当AI提升个体产出质量时，可能导致整体创意多样性崩溃。本文提出一个无需人机交互数据即可评估AI引发人类多样性塌缩风险的框架，通过将创意视为可拥挤资源，定义了超额拥挤系数Δ和相对多样性比率ρ，并在短故事、营销口号和替代用途任务上验证了前沿LLM均低于多样性平价线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI创意系统评估仅关注个体效用，但创意消费具有群体性——当大量AI生成相似内容时，个体创意价值会因拥挤而贬值。这种"评估盲点"使得AI可能在提升个体输出质量的同时加剧群体层面的同质化，而现有方法缺乏在AI部署前预判这种多样性塌缩风险的能力。

Method: 提出一个基于人类基线的相对评估框架：(1) 将创意建模为可拥挤资源，通过同分布比较识别源级拥挤；(2) 定义超额拥挤系数Δ和人类相对多样性比率ρ，其中ρ≥1为无超额拥挤的平价条件；(3) 将Δ与采纳博弈中的暴露依赖冗余成本相关联；(4) 仅需模型生成样本和匹配的无辅助人类基线数据，无需人机交互数据。

Result: 在短故事、营销口号和替代用途任务上，三个前沿LLM在所有拥挤核下均低于多样性平价线（ρ<1）。估计值在可行的模型纯样本量下保持稳定。此外，通过调整生成协议（如提示设计）可以显著降低拥挤程度。

Conclusion: 多样性塌缩是可操作的、可在开发阶段评估的优化目标。该框架为人口感知的创意AI提供了事前评估协议，且通过设计干预可以缓解拥挤问题，使AI系统的创意评估从个体导向转向群体生态视角。

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [159] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种确定性伴随匹配框架，将基于流的生成模型的人类偏好对齐转化为速度场上的最优控制问题，通过截断伴随方案和灵活正则化实现高效、高质量的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成模型在人类偏好对齐方面缺乏统一的理论框架，且计算成本高、正则化方式受限，亟需一种高效、稳定且灵活的对齐方法。

Method: 将偏好对齐建模为速度场上的最优控制问题，直接回归控制到当前策略下的值梯度诱导目标；引入截断伴随方案聚焦轨迹末端计算；推广至超越KL正则化的灵活正则化形式。

Result: 在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B上，多个对齐指标一致提升，同时大幅改善多样性和模态保持。

Conclusion: 该确定性伴随匹配框架为流模型偏好对齐提供了统一视角，在保持对齐质量的同时显著降低计算成本，并支持灵活的正则化权衡。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [160] [NeuroAgent: LLM Agents for Multimodal Neuroimaging Analysis and Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06584)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Jianwei Zhang,Shuo huang,Jiaxin Yue,Mingyang Xia,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroAgent是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动化处理多模态神经影像数据（sMRI、fMRI、dMRI、PET）的预处理和下游分析，在ADNI数据集（1,470名受试者）上四模态阿尔茨海默病分类达到AUC 0.9518，预处理步骤正确率最高达84.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经影像分析涉及复杂、模态特定的预处理流程，需要精心配置、质量控制和跨异构工具链的协调；此外，下游统计分析和疾病分类通常需要特定任务的代码、评估协议和数据格式约定，在原始数据采集和可重复科学分析之间造成了额外障碍。

Method: 采用分层多智能体架构，包含反馈驱动的Generate-Execute-Validate引擎；智能体自主生成可执行的预处理代码、检测并从运行时错误中恢复、验证输出完整性；支持通过自然语言查询进行交互式下游分析；集成了Human-In-The-Loop接口用于边缘情况的人工审查。

Result: 在ADNI全部阶段的1,470名受试者（CN=1,000, AD=470）上评估；最强后端Qwen3.5-27B实现84.8%端到端预处理步骤正确性，最高意图解析准确率达100%；四模态阿尔茨海默病分类AUC达0.9518，优于所有单模态基线。

Conclusion: NeuroAgent能够减少神经影像预处理所需的手动工作，为神经影像研究实现端到端的自动化分析流程。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging analysis often involves complex, modality-specific preprocessing workflows that require careful configuration, quality control, and coordination across heterogeneous toolchains. Beyond preprocessing, downstream statistical analysis and disease classification commonly require task-specific code, evaluation protocols, and data-format conventions, creating additional barriers between raw acquisitions and reproducible scientific analysis. We present NeuroAgent, an LLM-driven agentic framework that automates key preprocessing and analysis steps for heterogeneous neuroimaging data, including sMRI, fMRI, dMRI, and PET, and supports interactive downstream analysis through natural-language queries. NeuroAgent employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture with a feedback-driven Generate-Execute-Validate engine: agents autonomously generate executable preprocessing code, detect and recover from runtime errors, and validate output integrity. We evaluate the system on 1,470 subjects pooled across all ADNI phases (CN=1,000, AD=470), where all subjects have sMRI and tabular data, with subsets also having Tau-PET (n=469), fMRI (n=278), and DTI ($n=620$). Pipeline ablation studies across multiple LLM backends show that capable models reach up to 100% intent-parsing accuracy, with the strongest backend (Qwen3.5-27B) reaching 84.8% end-to-end preprocessing step correctness. Automated recovery limits manual intervention to edge cases where human review is required via the Human-In-The-Loop interface. For Alzheimer's Disease classification using automatically preprocessed multimodal data, our agent ensemble achieves an AUC of 0.9518 with four modalities, outperforming all single-modality baselines. These results show that NeuroAgent can reduce the manual effort required for neuroimaging preprocessing and enable end-to-end automated analysis pipelines for neuroimaging research.

</details>


### [161] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GlazyBench是首个面向AI辅助釉料设计的数据集，包含23,148个真实釉料配方，支持原料到烧成后表面性能预测及视觉生成两大任务，并建立了传统机器学习与大模型的基线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷釉料开发因复杂的化学性质而依赖于高成本、耗时的试错过程，给独立艺术家带来沉重负担。尽管多模态AI提供了现代解决方案，但缺乏大规模数据集来训练这些模型。

Method: 构建包含23,148个真实釉料配方的GlazyBench数据集，定义两个核心任务：从原料预测烧成后表面性能（颜色、透明度等），以及基于性能生成釉料视觉表征。使用传统机器学习和大语言模型建立属性预测基线，利用深度生成模型和大规模多模态模型建立图像生成基线。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法具有前景但仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: GlazyBench开创了AI辅助材料设计的新研究方向，为系统评估提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [162] [AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651)
*Daniel Zheng,Ingrid von Glehn,Yori Zwols,Iuliya Beloshapka,Lars Buesing,Daniel M. Roy,Martin Wattenberg,Bogdan Georgiev,Tatiana Schmidt,Andrew Cowie,Fernanda Viegas,Dimitri Kanevsky,Vineet Kahlon,Hartmut Maennel,Sophia Alj,George Holland,Alex Davies,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"AI 合作数学家"的工作台，允许数学家通过交互方式利用AI进行开放性数学研究，支持构思、文献搜索、计算探索、定理证明和理论构建，在早期测试中帮助解决开放问题，并在FrontierMath Tier 4上取得48%的新最高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI数学系统通常局限于单一任务（如定理证明或计算），缺乏对整个数学研究工作流（包括迭代探索、假设管理和理论构建）的全面支持，无法真正融入数学家的工作习惯。

Method: 构建了一个异步、有状态的工作台系统，能够管理不确定性、精炼用户意图、追踪失败假设并输出原生数学工件，通过人机协作的交互范式模拟人类合作研究流程。

Result: 在早期测试中帮助研究人员解决开放问题、发现新研究方向并找到被忽视的文献；在FrontierMath Tier 4基准测试上取得48%的成绩，在所有评估的AI系统中创下新高。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种高度交互的AI辅助数学发现范式，证明了AI合作数学家系统在开放式数学研究中的有效性，并在硬性问题求解基准上达到了最先进水平。

Abstract: We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251)
*Eric Wolos,Michael Doyle*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对二进制逆向工程中的函数关联问题，通过对比学习微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，实现了源码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联，在基线测试中大幅超越现有模型，并展现出对未见过的常量算法关联任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数关联搜索工具未充分利用AI搜索能力，且已有嵌入模型研究未覆盖源码与反编译剥离代码之间的双向关联，存在研究空白。

Method: 形式化定义了函数关联问题，采用对比学习（contrastive learning）微调Qwen3-Embedding模型，实现两种代码表示之间的双向嵌入关联。

Result: 微调后的模型在所有函数关联基线上以显著优势超越其他模型，并能泛化到未显式训练的常量算法关联任务上。

Conclusion: 对比学习微调的嵌入模型可有效解决源码与反编译剥离代码的双向关联问题，且具备良好的任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Function association is a useful process for binary reverse engineers. Search tools exist to perform association at scale, but they do not utilize the full range of capabilities that AI-enabled search provides. Prior work has explored the development of embedding models for association between certain reverse engineering code representations, but that work does not cover bidirectional association between source code and decompiled, stripped code with standard preprocessing requirements. To bridge this gap, we formalize this function association problem and evaluate the extent to which embedding models can bidirectionally associate between these two representations. To improve model performance at this task, we fine-tune a Qwen3-Embedding model with contrastive learning. We find that our new model outperforms other models on all function association baselines by a substantial margin and generalizes to a constant-algorithm association task it is not explicitly trained on.

</details>


### [164] [SecureMCP: A Policy-Enforced LLM Data Access Framework for AIoT Systems via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05260)
*Wonbae Kim,Hee-Kyong Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecureMCP框架，将基于角色的访问控制(RBAC)与MCP服务器集成，通过五个防御模块（check_policy、explain_gate、SQL Interceptor、Risk Level Filter、DB Isolation）形成顺序失败关闭流水线，为AIoT系统中LLM生成的SQL查询提供多层安全防护。实验表明在保持查询准确性的同时，对2,400个对抗性查询实现了82.3%的策略合规率和仅3.4%的真实防御失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的SQL查询在AIoT系统中存在严重安全风险，提示注入攻击可操纵LLM生成未授权查询，暴露敏感数据或执行破坏性操作。现有NL2SQL研究仅关注查询准确性，MCP服务器实现仅提供SQL级保护而缺乏细粒度的基于角色的访问控制。

Method: 提出SecureMCP框架，集成RBAC与MCP服务器，构建五个防御模块的顺序失败关闭流水线：check_policy（表/列级RBAC）、explain_gate（高代价查询阻断）、SQL Interceptor（危险模式检测）、Risk Level Filter（SQL风险分类）、DB Isolation（跨数据库限制），映射到OWASP Top 10 LLM应用的六种提示注入类型。

Result: 在IoT-SQL数据集上使用Qwen3-8B评估：防御模块保持执行准确性，EX-in-ALLOW在四个RBAC角色中维持在65.1%-76.4%，与未保护基线63.8%匹配；对2,400个对抗性查询达到82.3%策略合规率，真实防御失败率仅3.4%；check_policy贡献78.7%的阻断，次级模块额外提升17.5个百分点；注入归因率72.5%证实LLM高易感性。

Conclusion: SecureMCP通过多层外部策略强制机制有效防御LLM生成SQL的安全风险，验证了在AIoT系统中实施策略驱动数据访问框架的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Model (LLM)-generated SQL queries in Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems introduces critical security risks, as prompt injection attacks can manipulate LLMs into producing unauthorized queries that expose sensitive data or execute destructive operations. Existing NL2SQL research focuses on query accuracy, while MCP server implementations provide only SQL-level protections without fine-grained role-based access control. This paper proposes SecureMCP, a policy-enforced LLM data access framework integrating Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) with an MCP server to establish multi-layer defense for LLM-generated SQL execution. The framework incorporates five defense modules -- check_policy for table-and-column-level RBAC, explain_gate for cost-explosive query blocking, SQL Interceptor for dangerous pattern detection, Risk Level Filter for SQL risk classification, and DB Isolation for cross-database restriction -- operating in a sequential fail-closed pipeline mapped to six prompt injection types grounded in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. We evaluate SecureMCP on the IoT-SQL dataset (11 tables, 173 columns, 239,398 records) using Qwen3-8B. Experiment A demonstrates that defense modules preserve execution accuracy, with EX-in-ALLOW remaining within 65.1%-76.4% across four RBAC roles, matching the unprotected baseline of 63.8%. Experiment B shows that SecureMCP achieves 82.3% Policy Compliance on 2,400 adversarial queries, with genuine defense failures limited to 3.4%. The defense-in-depth analysis reveals check_policy accounts for 78.7% of blocks, while secondary modules contribute an additional 17.5 percentage-point improvement. The Injection Incorporation Rate of 72.5% confirms high LLM susceptibility, establishing the necessity of external policy enforcement.

</details>


### [165] [Differential Privacy in the Extensive-Form Bandit Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05266)
*Stephen Pasteris,Rahul Savani,Theodore Turocy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对扩展式博弈中的在线学习问题（extensive-form bandit），提出了首个满足ε-局部差分隐私的算法，在T轮博弈中实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A为总动作数，S为简化策略数，每轮时间复杂度与传输简化策略所需时间相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展式博弈中的在线学习问题尚未有研究涉及差分隐私保护，而局部差分隐私作为最强形式的差分隐私，在实际应用中（如服务器协调用户决策的场景）具有重要隐私保护意义。

Method: 提出了一种满足ε-局部差分隐私的扩展式博弈在线学习算法，每轮算法的时间复杂度（除对数因子外）等于服务器向用户传输简化策略所需的时间。

Result: 算法实现了$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$的遗憾界，其中A是学习者可能采取的总动作数，S是学习者的可能简化策略数，T是试验轮数。

Conclusion: 本文首次将差分隐私引入扩展式博弈在线学习问题，并给出了在最强隐私保护级别（局部差分隐私）下的有效算法，遗憾界与隐私预算ε呈反比关系。

Abstract: We consider the extensive-form bandit problem, where on each trial the learner (a user coordinated by a server) plays an extensive-form game against an oblivious adversary, observing the information sets it finds itself in as well as the resulting payoff/loss. We give an algorithm for this problem that satisfies $ε$-local differential privacy and attains a regret of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{A\ln(S)T}/ε)$, where $A$ is the total number of actions that the learner can possibly take, $S$ is the number of the learner's possible reduced strategies, and $T$ is the number of trials. On each trial, the time complexity of our algorithm is, up to a factor logarithmic in the maximum number of actions at an infoset, equal to the time required for the server to transmit the reduced strategy to the user. We note that local differential privacy is the strongest version of differential privacy and, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study differential privacy of any form in the extensive-form bandit problem.

</details>


### [166] [Dynamic Authorization for Knowledge-Base Agents in 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05269)
*Loay Abdelrazek,Leyli Karacay,Marin Orlic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种面向6G多智能体系统的混合授权框架，结合角色和一阶逻辑谓词，在知识图谱的三元组级别实施零信任原则，解决传统RBAC模型在语义数据访问控制中的粒度不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G架构向去中心化多智能体系统演进，智能体需要安全访问共享知识库，但传统RBAC等授权模型无法为基于语义数据的自主智能体提供足够的访问控制粒度。

Method: 提出混合授权框架，将角色与一阶逻辑谓词相结合，在三元组级别（主体-谓词-客体）实施授权，消除权限继承，确保智能体仅访问其功能生命周期所需的元数据。

Result: 在知识图谱层面实现了零信任原则的细粒度访问控制，智能体仅能访问其功能生命周期所需的特定元数据。

Conclusion: 该混合授权框架能够为6G去中心化多智能体系统提供比传统RBAC更精细的语义数据访问控制，通过三元组级别的零信任授权机制保障知识库安全。

Abstract: As 6G architectures transition toward decentralized Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), ensuring secure access to shared Knowledge Bases (KB) is critical. Traditional authorization models like RBAC fail to provide the granularity required for autonomous agents interacting with Semantic-based data. This work proposes a hybrid authorization framework that integrates roles and First-Order Logic (FOL) predicates to enforce zero-trust principles at the knowledge-graph level. We eliminate permission inheritance by enforcing authorization at the triple level (Subject-Predicate-Object), ensuring agents only access metadata required for their specific functional lifecycle.

</details>


### [167] [Shattering the Echo Chamber: Hidden Safeguards in Manuscripts Against the AI Takeover of Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05271)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Yong Yang,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IntraGuard，一种基于PDF结构-视觉解耦的黑盒防御框架，通过向稿件中嵌入防御性文本对象，检测或干扰LLM完全外包的同行评审，在7个商业聊天机器人设置和12个学术 venues 上实现最高84%的防御成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力增强，编辑委员会越来越担心审稿人将同行评审完全外包给商业聊天机器人（端到端评审外包），而现有防御方法因依赖同质化载荷且采用流间注入方式，容易被清洗或中和。

Method: 提出IntraGuard，一种黑盒、与会议无关的防御框架。利用PDF固有的结构-视觉解耦特性，通过三种流内注入机制在PDF底层结构中嵌入异质防御文本对象（不改变视觉呈现），支持显式策略（触发拒绝或警告信号）和隐式策略（嵌入预定义文本标记）。

Result: 在7个真实商业聊天机器人设置和12个跨学科venues上评估，防御成功率最高达84%；对人工审稿人保持同行评审不变性；平均每篇稿件仅增加1秒开销（普通个人电脑）；评估了11种自适应攻击并讨论了集成防御的含义。

Conclusion: IntraGuard是一种轻量级、硬件无关的有效防御框架，能够在不影响人工审稿体验的前提下检测并防御LLM完全外包评审行为，为维护学术评审完整性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly capable, editorial boards and program committees are growing concerned about reviewers who fully outsource peer review to commercial chatbots. This concern stems from prior findings that current chatbots lack the independent critical thinking and depth of reasoning required to assess scientific novelty. One promising direction for mitigating this concern is to embed hidden instructions into manuscripts that disrupt or alter chatbot-generated reviews. However, existing methods remain intuitive and fragile, as they typically rely on homogeneous payloads injected in an inter-stream manner, rendering them susceptible to sanitization or neutralization.
  In this paper, we identify End-to-End Review Outsourcing as an emerging threat and propose IntraGuard, a black-box, venue-agnostic defense framework grounded in the structural--visual decoupling inherent to the PDF. Designed for committee-side deployment, IntraGuard supports both explicit strategies that trigger refusal or warning signals, and implicit strategies that embed predefined textual markers into the generated review. These strategies can be deployed via any of three intra-stream injection mechanisms, each of which seamlessly embeds heterogeneous defensive text objects within the PDF's underlying structure without altering its visual presentation. Extensive evaluations across 7 real-world commercial chatbot settings and 12 venues spanning diverse disciplines show that IntraGuard achieves a defense success rate of up to 84%, while preserving peer-review invariance for human reviewers. IntraGuard is lightweight and hardware-independent, incurring an average overhead of only one second per manuscript on a commodity personal computer. We further evaluate 11 adaptive attacks spanning manuscript sanitization and instruction interference, and discuss the implications of constructing ensemble defenses.

</details>


### [168] [Sealing the Audit-Runtime Gap for LLM Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05274)
*Tingda Shen,Yebo Feng,Konglin Zhu,Xiaojun Jia,Yang Liu,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SIGIL 是首个弥合 LLM 技能审计与运行时之间鸿沟的框架，通过不可篡改的去中心化链上注册表实现可验证托管，并由嵌入的 Skill Verification Loader (SVL) 在加载时强制执行完整性验证与权限清单，批处理验证耗时低于 86 毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 生态系统（如 Claude Code、ChatGPT）越来越依赖技能包（自然语言指令+可执行工具），但技能内容无法与可信指令可靠分离，且可执行侧可调用特权操作，导致技能供应链面临注入、篡改和 rug-pull 攻击。现有防御存在阶段局限：中心化签名、脱离运行时工件的审计报告、无法证明被批准内容的策略引擎。

Method: SIGIL 提供可验证托管：通过不可篡改的去中心化链上注册表直接获取技能，支持四种发布类型（Transparent、Licensed、Sealed、Committed）；由 DAO 审计委员会在准入前对技能进行审查，采用 stake-and-slash 经济模型；加载时通过嵌入的 Skill Verification Loader (SVL) 执行技能验证协议，按类型检索/解密技能、验证链上记录完整性、强制执行权限清单后再注入上下文。

Result: 对 1,023 个真实世界技能（覆盖六种攻击类型）的评估显示，SVL 可在 86 毫秒内完成批量验证，证明 LLM 技能可以从发布到运行时实现密码学绑定，且开销实用可行。

Conclusion: SIGIL 首次弥合了 LLM 技能审计到运行时的安全鸿沟，通过链上注册表与验证加载器实现了端到端可验证性，证明在实用成本下即可实现密码学绑定的技能供应链安全。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) ecosystems such as Claude Code and ChatGPT increasingly rely on skills: packages of natural-language instructions and executable tools. Once in the LLM's context, skill content cannot be reliably separated from trusted instructions, and a skill's executable side can invoke privileged actions, exposing the skill supply chain to injection, tampering, and rug-pull attacks. Existing defenses are stage-bound: centralized signing, audit reports unbound from the runtime artifact, or policy engines that cannot attest to what was approved. We present SIGIL, the first framework that seals the audit-runtime gap for LLM skills. SIGIL delivers verifiable hosting through a tamper-evident, decentralized on-chain registry from which LLMs fetch skills directly. The registry admits four publication types, Transparent, Licensed, Sealed, and Committed, spanning plaintext public distribution, monetized access, custodial use, and off-chain workflows; before admission, every skill is vetted by a Decentralized Autonomous Organization (DAO) audit committee that supports pluggable auditing methods under a stake-and-slash economic model. At load time, SIGIL delivers verified loading through a skill verification protocol executed by a Skill Verification Loader (SVL) embedded as the mandatory loading path: the SVL retrieves and decrypts the skill as its type requires, verifies its integrity against the on-chain record, and enforces its permission manifest before context injection. We evaluate SIGIL on a real-world deployment against 1,023 in-the-wild skills spanning six attack types. At load time, the SVL verifies each skill's integrity against its on-chain record and enforces its approved permission manifest, completing batched verification under 86 ms. Together, these results show that LLM skills can be cryptographically bound from publication through runtime at practical cost.

</details>


### [169] [GLiNER Guard: Unified Encoder Family for Production LLM Safety and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05277)
*Bogdan Minko,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一种统一编码器，可在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测，在严格的延迟和成本约束下实现高效推理。紧凑型号（145-147M参数）在单A100上达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍。同时发布PII-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级LLM系统需要在严格的延迟和成本约束下同时进行安全审核和PII检测。自回归审核器准确但昂贵，轻量级编码器更快但能力不足，存在权衡。作者希望设计一种统一的编码器方案，在保持效率的同时兼顾准确性。

Method: 提出GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard)，一种统一的编码器模型，包含三个变体：紧凑型单/双编码器（145-147M参数）用于高吞吐量服务，以及GLiGuard Omni（209M参数）用于更强的审核质量。模型在单次前向传播中同时完成安全分类和PII检测。

Result: 在动态批处理的单A100上，紧凑模型达到193请求/秒，P99延迟低于1秒，吞吐量比GLiNER2高1.6倍。Omni在公共安全基准上与更大的审核模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 基于编码器的护栏为始终在线的审核提供了一种实用的低成本替代方案，能够在不牺牲延迟和吞吐量的情况下统一完成安全分类和PII检测。

Abstract: Production LLM systems require both safety moderation and PII detection under strict latency and cost constraints. This creates a trade-off: autoregressive moderators are accurate but expensive, while lightweight encoders are faster but less capable. We present GLiNER Guard (GLiGuard), a unified encoder that performs safety classification and PII detection in a single forward pass, simplifying safety pipelines. We introduce three variants: compact uni- and bi-encoders (145-147M) for high-throughput serving, and GLiGuard Omni (209M) for stronger moderation quality. Under dynamic batching on a single A100, the compact model reaches 193 requests/sec with P99 latency below 1s, achieving 1.6x higher throughput than GLiNER2. Omni remains competitive with much larger moderators on public safety benchmarks. We also release PII-Bench, a span-level benchmark for evaluating PII detection in end-to-end pipelines. Overall, encoder-based guardrails offer a practical low-cost alternative for always-on moderation. Models and benchmarks are released on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [170] [Securing the Agent: Vendor-Neutral, Multitenant Enterprise Retrieval and Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05287)
*Francisco Javier Arceo,Varsha Prasad Narsing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多租户企业级RAG和智能体系统的分层隔离架构，通过策略感知的接入、检索时门控和共享推理，结合服务端编排，在OGX框架中实现并验证了其消除跨租户数据泄露的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和智能体系统在真实企业环境中面临多租户、异构数据、严格访问控制和合规等挑战，而传统检索系统仅按相关性排序而非授权，导致跨租户数据泄露风险。工具介导的披露、跨轮上下文积累和客户端编排绕过等问题进一步加剧了安全缺口。

Method: 提出分层隔离架构：1) 策略感知的接入（文档解析时注入访问控制元数据）；2) 检索时门控（基于ABAC的检索后过滤，去除未授权文档）；3) 共享推理（服务端多轮编排集中执行工具调用授权、状态隔离和策略执行）。在OGX框架中实现OpenAI兼容的Responses API作为开源验证。

Result: 实验表明，ABAC门控在消除跨租户数据泄露的同时引入的计算开销可忽略不计（negligible overhead），验证了该架构在真实企业场景中的可行性和效率。

Conclusion: 将安全关键操作集中到服务端、同时保持客户端对智能体组合和延迟敏感操作的控制权，是解决多租户企业级RAG/智能体系统安全隔离问题的有效路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic AI systems are increasingly prevalent in enterprise AI deployments. However, real enterprise environments introduce challenges largely absent from academic treatments and consumer-facing APIs: multiple tenants with heterogeneous data, strict access-control requirements, regulatory compliance, and cost pressures that demand shared infrastructure.
  A fundamental problem underlies existing RAG architectures in these settings: retrieval systems rank documents by relevance--whether through semantic similarity, keyword matching, or hybrid approaches--not by authorization, so a query from one tenant can surface another tenant's confidential data simply because it scores highest. We formalize this gap and analyze additional shortcomings--including tool-mediated disclosure, context accumulation across turns, and client-side orchestration bypass--that arise when agentic systems conflate relevance with authorization. To address these challenges, we introduce a layered isolation architecture combining policy-aware ingestion, retrieval-time gating, and shared inference, enforced through server-side agentic orchestration. This approach centralizes security-critical operations--tool execution authorization, state isolation, and policy enforcement--on the server, creating natural enforcement points for multitenant isolation while allowing client-side frameworks to retain control over agent composition and latency-sensitive operations.
  We validate the proposed architecture through an open-source implementation in OGX, a vendor-neutral framework that implements an OpenAI-compatible, open-source Responses API with server-side multi-turn orchestration. We evaluate it empirically and show that ABAC gating eliminates cross-tenant leakage while introducing negligible overhead.

</details>


### [171] [How Far Are VLMs from Privacy Awareness in the Physical World? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05340)
*Junran Wang,Xinjie Shen,Zehao Jin,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ImmersedPrivacy，一个基于Unity模拟器的交互式视听评估框架，用于评估VLM在物理环境中的隐私意识。测试12个SOTA模型发现：场景复杂度增加时性能单调下降、社交情境变化时选择准确率不超过65%、面对冲突指令时最佳模型仅51%能平衡任务完成与隐私保护。揭示当前VLM存在感知脆弱性，无法让隐私知识指导情境行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM日益被部署为具身助手的自主认知核心，需要在物理环境中评估其隐私意识。现有基准局限于单模态、文本化表示，无法捕捉真实世界场景中的隐私挑战，特别是在家庭、医院等私密空间中VLM具备观察和操作敏感信息的物理能力。

Method: 构建ImmersedPrivacy框架，基于Unity模拟器模拟真实物理环境；设计三个递进测试层级：杂乱场景中识别敏感物品、适应社交情境变化、解决显式指令与隐式隐私约束之间的冲突；评估12个SOTA VLM模型。

Result: 杂乱场景中所有模型随复杂度增加出现单调性能衰减（感知缺陷）；社交情境变化时无模型选择准确率超过65%；冲突指令下最佳模型gemini-3.1-pro仅在51%案例中完美平衡任务完成与隐私保护。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在物理世界中存在感知脆弱性，且无法让隐私知识指导情境行为，距离可靠部署于真实物理环境仍有显著差距。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous cognitive cores for embodied assistants, evaluating their privacy awareness in physical environments becomes critical. Unlike digital chatbots, these agents operate in intimate spaces, such as homes and hospitals, where they possess the physical agency to observe and manipulate privacy-sensitive information and artifacts. However, current benchmarks remain limited to unimodal, text-based representations that cannot capture the demands of real-world settings. To bridge this gap, we present ImmersedPrivacy, an interactive audio-visual evaluation framework that simulates realistic physical environments using a Unity-based simulator. ImmersedPrivacy evaluates physically grounded privacy awareness across three progressive tiers that test a model's ability to identify sensitive items in cluttered scenes, adapt to shifting social contexts, and resolve conflicts between explicit commands and inferred privacy constraints. Our evaluation of 12 state-of-the-art models reveals consistent deficits. In cluttered scenes, all models exhibit monotonic performance decay as scene complexity grows due to perceptual deficit. When social context shifts, no model exceed 65% selection accuracy. Under conflicting commands, the best model gemini-3.1-pro perfectly balances task completion and privacy preservation in only 51% of cases. These findings reveal that current VLMs in the physical world suffer from perceptual fragility and fail to let their knowledge of privacy cues govern their situated behavior. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/immersed-privacy/immersed-privacy .

</details>


### [172] [Privacy Without Losing Place: A Paradigm for Private Retrieval in Spatial RAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05459)
*Kennedy Edemacu,Mohammad Mahdi Shokri,Vinay M. Shashidhar,Jong Wook Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PAS（隐私锚点替换）机制，通过相对锚点编码（锚点、方向箱、距离箱）实现空间RAG系统中的用户位置隐私保护，在合成城市数据集上达到约370-400米对抗定位误差的同时保留超过50%的检索性能，且下游生成质量保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方法直接扰动用户位置，难以与RAG管道无缝集成。需要一种结构化的位置隐私机制，既能兼容现代RAG流程，又能提供可量化的隐私保障。

Method: 提出PAS（Privacy Anchor Substitution），将用户位置表示为相对锚点编码，包含三个组件：锚点（anchor）、方向箱（direction bin）和距离箱（distance bin），从而替代原始位置坐标与RAG系统交互。

Result: 在合成城市数据集上达到约370-400米的对抗定位误差；保留超过50%的基线检索性能；下游生成质量保持鲁棒；发现隐私-效用关系呈非单调性，归因于锚点离散化引入的几何偏差。

Conclusion: 结构化空间表示为RAG系统中基于位置的推理提供了一种实用的隐私保护途径；大语言模型能够补偿不完美的空间检索；PAS区别于连续噪声机制（如geo-indistinguishability）的非单调隐私-效用关系值得关注。

Abstract: This work introduces PAS -- Privacy Anchor Substitution, a structured mechanism for enabling user location privacy in spatial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Unlike conventional differential privacy methods that directly perturb user locations, PAS represents location with relative anchor encoding consisting of an anchor, direction bin, and distance bin, allowing seamless integration with modern RAG pipelines. We evaluate PAS on a synthetic urban dataset and show that it achieves impressive coarse privacy guarantees, with approximately 370-400m adversarial location error, while retaining more than half of the baseline retrieval performance. Despite the slight drop in retrieval performance, the downstream generation quality under PAS remains comparatively robust, indicating that large language models can compensate for imperfect spatial retrieval. Furthermore, we provide empirical analysis showing that PAS exhibits non-monotonic privacy-utility relationship with respect to privacy parameters. We attribute this to geometric bias induced by anchor discretization, making it different from continuous noise mechanisms such as geo-indistinguishability. Our results show that structured spatial representations offer a practical approach to privacy in location based reasoning in RAG systems.

</details>


### [173] [SOCpilot: Verifying Policy Compliance for LLM-Assisted Incident Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05501)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Leonardo Vaz de Meneses,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SOCpilot是一个用于验证LLM生成的应急响应计划合规性的可测量框架。通过对200个真实安全事件的分析，它发现两个LLM提供商在不同方向上受到相同策略文本的影响；确定性验证器移除了466个不合规操作而未降低召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）开始使用大语言模型（LLM）作为辅助工具来起草应急响应计划，但这些计划中的操作可能在目录层面有效，却违反强制步骤、必要顺序或审批关卡，导致合规性问题尚不可测量。

Method: SOCpilot通过固定事件包、操作目录、策略规则、验证器和公开证据表面，在计划边界处量化合规性。它验证LLM copilot提出的操作轨迹，并在一个金融部门生产SOC的200个真实事件上评估两个LLM提供商，将结果与分析师编写的参考计划进行比较。

Result: 相同的内联策略文本使两个LLM提供商向相反方向移动；确定性验证器移除了466个不合规的、需要审批的操作而不降低基线任务召回率；聚合率在固定语料库的3次重复运行中保持稳定；官方证据聚焦于恢复和遏制相关的审批决策。

Conclusion: SOCpilot使LLM在SOC中的合规性问题变得可测量，同时公开发布可运行工件，使独立评审者无需访问私有事件数据即可重现公开结果。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) are beginning to use large language models (LLMs) as copilots to draft incident-response plans. These plans may include actions that are valid per the catalog but still violate mandatory steps, required ordering, or approval gates before analyst review. SOCpilot makes this compliance question measurable at the plan boundary. It fixes the incident package, action catalog, policy rules, verifier, and public evidence surface. Next, it verifies the copilot's proposed action trace. We evaluate two LLM providers on 200 real incidents from an anonymized production SOC in a financial-sector case study. We compare their plans to paired analyst-authored references from the same security orchestration, automation, and response (SOAR) cases. An identical inline policy text moves the two providers in opposite directions. A deterministic verifier removes 466 non-compliant, approval-gated actions, without reducing baseline-task recall. Aggregate rates remain stable across 3 reruns of the fixed corpus. The official evidence focuses on approval-gated decisions regarding recovery and containment. Separately, the artifact exposes zero-cost readiness checks for mandatory and ordering repairs. We release the runnable artifact so independent reviewers can rederive the public results without access to private incident data.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Collection: Measuring the Detection Efficacy of Modern Security Logging Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05531)
*Ryan Holeman,John Hastings,Varghese Mathew Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了CIM、OCSF和ECS三种主流安全日志标准在检测远程代码执行漏洞方面的有效性，通过自动化的SETC框架在容器化环境中生成50个可复现的漏洞利用场景，量化了各标准的遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性，发现不同日志标准在捕获关键攻击指标方面存在显著差异，为安全从业者提供了基于证据的日志标准选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对不同安全日志标准在实际漏洞检测中有效性的系统性评估，安全从业者在选择日志标准时缺乏数据驱动的决策依据。

Method: 提出自动化SETC框架，在容器化环境中系统生成可复现的漏洞利用场景，收集CIM、OCSF和ECS三种日志标准的遥测数据，通过遥测完整性和漏洞可检测性两个指标对50个远程代码执行漏洞进行量化评估。

Result: 不同日志标准在捕获关键攻击指标方面存在显著差异，揭示了各标准在安全检测中的能力差距。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可扩展、可复现的漏洞利用遥测评估方法论，并为安全从业者选择日志标准提供了清晰的、基于证据的实践指导。

Abstract: Effective security logging is crucial for the timely and accurate detection of cyber threats; however, the relative effectiveness of various industry-standard logging frameworks remains understudied. This paper addresses this critical gap by presenting the first systematic evaluation of modern security logging standards utilizing a novel methodology built upon the automated Security Exploit Telemetry Collection (SETC) framework. SETC systematically generates reproducible exploit scenarios in containerized environments, collecting rich telemetry across multiple logging standards, including CIM (Common Information Model), OCSF (Open Cybersecurity Schema Framework), and ECS (Elastic Common Schema). The detection efficacy of each logging standard is quantified by measuring telemetry completeness and exploit detectability across standardized logs through detailed experiments involving 50 diverse remote code execution vulnerabilities. The resulting findings identify critical gaps and reveal significant differences in logging standards' abilities to capture key attack indicators. Our contributions include a novel evaluation methodology that enables scalable and reproducible analysis of exploit telemetry, as well as new findings that provide clear, evidence-based guidance for security practitioners to make informed decisions about adopting logging standards.

</details>


### [175] [Architecture Matters: Comparing RAG Systems under Knowledge Base Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05632)
*Samuel Korn*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了四种RAG架构（Vanilla RAG、Agentic RAG、MADAM-RAG、Recursive Language Model）在面对知识库投毒攻击（CorruptRAG-AK）时的对抗鲁棒性。研究发现，架构是影响鲁棒性的关键变量：攻击成功率从Vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，差距近58个百分点。对抗性框架（而非检索优化）是攻击优势的主要来源，定位了跨架构的脆弱性在于内容推理阶段。MADAM-RAG虽有最高矛盾检测率但精度低（约48.5%），且即使检测到也无法可靠解决矛盾，在干净输入上产生41.4%的不作答率。论文还引入了七类行为分类体系。（N=1单文档投毒，921个Natural Questions QA对）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对RAG系统的知识库投毒攻击评估几乎全部局限于vanilla retrieve-then-generate流水线，而设计用于处理冲突检索信息的高级架构（多智能体辩论、智能体检索、递归语言模型）尚未经过对抗优化矛盾的测试，存在评估空白。

Method: 在921个Natural Questions QA对上、单文档（N=1）投毒设置下，对比四种RAG架构在干净基线、朴素注入和CorruptRAG-AK攻击下的表现。通过分解攻击优势来源（检索优化 vs. 对抗性框架），定位脆弱性环节。引入七类行为分类体系进行细粒度分析。

Result: 架构是对抗鲁棒性的高影响力变量：CorruptRAG-AK下攻击成功率从Vanilla的81.9%到RLM的24.4%，跨架构差距近58个百分点，而干净准确率均约92%。对抗性框架（而非检索优化）驱动了三种架构中CorruptRAG-AK的大部分优势。MADAM-RAG矛盾检测率最高但精度仅~48.5%，且干净输入上非答案率41.4%。

Conclusion: 不同RAG架构在对抗鲁棒性上存在巨大差异，核心脆弱性在于内容推理阶段而非检索阶段。即使设计用于处理冲突信息的架构（如MADAM-RAG）也无法可靠解决矛盾，可能以牺牲可用性（高非答案率）为代价。需要超越二元准确率的细粒度评估方法。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to knowledge base poisoning, yet existing attacks have been evaluated almost exclusively against vanilla retrieve-then-generate pipelines. Architectures designed to handle conflicting retrieved information - multi-agent debate, agentic retrieval, recursive language models - remain untested against adversarially optimized contradictions. We evaluate four RAG architectures (vanilla RAG, agentic RAG, MADAM-RAG, and Recursive Language Models) under controlled single-document (N=1) poisoning on 921 Natural Questions QA pairs, comparing a clean baseline, naive injection, and CorruptRAG-AK - an adversarial attack whose meta-epistemic framing targets credibility assessment. Architecture is a high-impact variable in adversarial robustness: under CorruptRAG-AK, attack success rates range from 81.9% (vanilla) to 24.4% (RLM) - a spread of nearly 58 percentage points across architectures with comparable clean accuracy (~92%). Decomposing this gap, once the poisoned document is retrieved, adversarial framing - not retrieval optimization - drives the majority of CorruptRAG-AK's advantage for three of four architectures, localizing the cross-architecture vulnerability at the content-reasoning stage. Our MADAM-RAG reimplementation shows the highest apparent contradiction detection rate, though our LLM judge over-identifies this behavior (~48.5% precision), so reported rates are upper bounds. Regardless of detection, MADAM-RAG cannot resolve contradictions reliably, producing a 41.4% non-answer rate even on clean inputs - though implementation divergences from the original may contribute. We introduce a seven-category behavioral taxonomy capturing contradiction detection, hedging, and failure modes beyond binary accuracy. Code, data, and analysis notebooks are publicly available.

</details>


### [176] [AoI-Guided Client Selection for Robust and Timely Federated Intrusion Detection in Cloud-Edge Security Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05644)
*Chun Yin Chiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在云边入侵检测中的客户端参与问题，提出基于信息年龄（AoI）的三种轻量级客户端选择策略（AoI优先、效用优先、混合AoI+效用），在多种数据集和攻击场景下，AoI感知选择可将平均AoI降低39-41%、峰值AoI降低约70%，同时保持检测性能，为实现时效性优化的联邦入侵检测提供了轻量级调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云边联邦入侵检测的生产环境中，仅有部分客户端参与每轮训练，异构带宽、掉队者和掉线导致服务器依赖过时客户端信息，影响模型时效性和检测质量。现有研究未将客户端参与问题作为时效性感知的系统问题加以研究，缺乏轻量级的调度策略来优化信息新鲜度。

Method: 将客户端参与问题建模为时效性感知的系统问题，利用信息年龄（AoI）度量客户端信息新鲜度。设计了三种轻量级客户端选择策略：AoI优先（选最旧信息）、效用优先（选最大梯度范数）、混合AoI+效用策略（可调权衡参数）。在CIC-IDS2017、NSL-KDD、ToN-IoT和合成漂移基准数据集上，于干净、投毒、投毒+鲁棒聚合三种设置下进行实验评估。

Result: 相较于随机采样，AoI感知选择在固定通信预算下平均AoI降低约39-41%，峰值AoI降低约70%。混合策略通常保持Macro-F1/AUC性能，并提供可解释的权衡旋钮用于平衡新鲜度、检测质量和鲁棒性，但在加入假阳性率指标后并非均匀帕累托占优。结合修剪均值聚合可抵御标签翻转投毒攻击。

Conclusion: 云边隐私保护的入侵分析可通过轻量级调度层改善时效性，无需改变底层联邦参与预算；AoI引导的选择策略本身不是独立的拜占庭防御机制，但可与鲁棒聚合结合使用。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is attractive for cloud-edge intrusion detection because it enables collaborative training over distributed telemetry without centralizing raw logs. In production security analytics pipelines, however, only a subset of clients participates in each round, and heterogeneous bandwidth, stragglers, and dropouts can cause the server to rely on stale client information. This paper studies client participation as a timeliness-aware systems problem using Age of Information (AoI). We compare three lightweight policies for federated intrusion detection: AoI-first, utility-first, and a hybrid AoI+utility rule with a tunable trade-off parameter.
  Across a CIC-IDS2017 DDoS/PortScan mini subset, NSL-KDD, ToN-IoT, and a synthetic drift benchmark under clean, poisoning, and poisoning-plus-robust-aggregation settings, AoI-aware selection reduces average AoI by about 39--41% and peak AoI by about 70% relative to random sampling while keeping the per-round communication budget fixed. The hybrid policy usually preserves Macro-F1/AUC and provides an interpretable knob for balancing freshness, detection quality, and robustness, although it is not uniformly Pareto-dominant once false positive rate is included. Robustness is evaluated by combining AoI-guided selection with trimmed-mean aggregation under label-flip poisoning; the selection policy itself is not intended as a standalone Byzantine defense. The main practical message is that cloud-edge, privacy-preserving intrusion analytics can improve timeliness through a lightweight scheduling layer without changing the underlying FL participation budget.

</details>


### [177] [SafeHarbor: Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Guardrail for LLM Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05704)
*Zhe Liu,Zonghao Ying,Wenxin Zhang,Quanchen Zou,Deyue Zhang,Dongdong Yang,Xiangzheng Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeHarbor是一个针对LLM Agent的防御框架，通过上下文感知的防御规则、局部层次记忆系统和基于信息熵的自演化机制，在安全性和实用性之间取得平衡，无需训练即可即插即用，在GPT-4o上实现了63.6%的良性任务效用和93%以上的有害请求拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent强大的工具调用能力带来了安全风险，现有防御机制存在过度拒绝（over-refusal）问题，即提高安全严格性会损害agent在良性任务上的效用。作者旨在解决安全性与实用性之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出SafeHarbor框架：(1) 通过增强对抗生成提取上下文感知的防御规则；(2) 设计局部层次记忆系统用于动态规则注入，无需训练、即插即用；(3) 引入基于信息熵的自演化机制，通过动态节点分裂与合并持续优化记忆结构。

Result: 在GPT-4o上良性任务效用达到63.6%，同时有害请求拒绝率维持在93%以上，实现了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: SafeHarbor有效缓解了安全性与实用性之间的权衡问题，为LLM Agent提供了一种高效、可扩展的防御方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of foundation models, Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated increasingly powerful tool-use capabilities. However, this proficiency introduces significant security risks, as malicious actors can manipulate agents into executing tools to generate harmful content. While existing defensive mechanisms are effective, they frequently suffer from the over-refusal problem, where increased safety strictness compromises the agent's utility on benign tasks. To mitigate this trade-off, we propose \textsc{SafeHarbor}, a novel framework designed to establish precise decision boundaries for LLM agents. Unlike static guidelines, \textsc{SafeHarbor} extracts context-aware defense rules through enhanced adversarial generation. We design a local hierarchical memory system for dynamic rule injection, offering a training-free, efficient, and plug-and-play solution. Furthermore, we introduce an information entropy-based self-evolution mechanism that continuously optimizes the memory structure through dynamic node splitting and merging. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textsc{SafeHarbor} achieves state-of-the-art performance on both ambiguous benign tasks and explicit malicious attacks, notably attaining a peak benign utility of 63.6\% on GPT-4o while maintaining a robust refusal rate exceeding 93\% against harmful requests. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ljj-cyber/SafeHarbor.

</details>


### [178] [$α$-Wasserstein Mechanism for Rényi Pufferfish Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05723)
*Ni Ding,Wenjin Yang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出α- Wasserstein机制，利用Hölder不等式将Laplace和Gaussian噪声的尺度参数通过W_α度量上界进行校准，实现对Rényi Pufferfish Privacy的精确满足。该框架统一了W_∞机制，推广了Rényi差分隐私，并在实验上显著降低了噪声功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Pufferfish隐私框架主要基于W_∞度量（对应ε-Pufferfish隐私），缺乏对Rényi散度变体的系统性研究；同时传统方法依赖δ-近似松弛，无法实现精确隐私保证。本文旨在建立基于W_α度量的通用框架，统一并推广现有机制。

Method: 1）利用Hölder不等式推导Laplace机制中尺度参数与W_α度量上界的关系，满足(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy；2）证明α=∞时退化为W_∞机制；3）将结果扩展至指数机制；4）提出Gaussian噪声的W_α机制，推广Rényi差分隐私结果。

Result: 提出的α-Wasserstein机制相比W_∞机制显著降低噪声功率，Gaussian机制优于Laplace机制；所有机制均实现精确的(α, ε)-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy，无需δ-近似松弛。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Rényi Pufferfish Privacy的α-Wasserstein理论框架，提供了Laplace与Gaussian两种实用机制，在保持严格隐私保证的同时提升了数据效用，为隐私保护数据发布提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces the $α$-Wasserstein mechanism for achieving Rényi Pufferfish Privacy using Laplace and Gaussian noise. By leveraging Hölder's inequality, we demonstrate that the scale parameter of the Laplace mechanism can be calibrated via an upper bound on the $W_α$ metric to satisfy $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy for $α\in (1, \infty]$. We show that at the limit $α= \infty$, this framework recovers the established $W_\infty$ mechanism for $ε$-pufferfish privacy. This result is subsequently extended to the exponential mechanism. Furthermore, we propose a $W_α$ mechanism for Gaussian noise for $α\in (1, \infty)$, demonstrating that it generalizes existing results within the Rényi Differential Privacy framework. Experimental evaluations reveal that our $α$-Wasserstein mechanism significantly reduces noise power compared to the conventional $W_\infty$-based approach, with the Gaussian mechanism providing superior utility over the Laplace mechanism. Notably, the mechanisms derived in this work achieve exact $(α, ε)$-Rényi Pufferfish Privacy without requiring additional relaxations, such as $δ$-approximations.

</details>


### [179] [SuperPaymaster: Eliminating Centralized Signer Authority via Asset-Oriented Abstraction to Reconcile Usability and Decentralization in Account Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05774)
*Huifeng Jiao,Nathapon Udomlertsakul*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为资产导向抽象（AOA）的新型ERC-4337支付主架构，通过链上"Gas卡"资产替代传统中心化离线签名流程，在Optimism主网上实现去中心化赞助，相比商业方案可降低49%的L2执行Gas成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ERC-4337支付主多采用流程导向抽象（POA），依赖中心化离线服务器签署赞助请求，构成潜在审查瓶颈和中心化风险。

Method: 基于设计科学研究方法，在Optimism主网实现SuperPaymaster，将赞助有效性锚定在链上Soulbound Token状态和确定性策略规则中，移除离线签名者作为硬性验证关卡。通过单UserOp ERC-20转账实验（每组n=50）评估Gas成本，采用跟踪级分解隔离执行成本差异。

Result: 消除中心化签名带来约32k Gas的执行成本增量；纯L2执行Gas方面，SuperPaymaster（167,830）相比基于DEX路由的ERC-20基线（328,937）降低49%；总计费Gas差距主要由打包者定价而非支付主架构决定；故障切换模拟表明在存在替代中继者时可绕过非协作中继者。

Conclusion: 资产导向抽象（AOA）能够降低可用性、赞助去中心化和经济效率之间的张力，为ERC-4337支付主提供更优的架构选择。

Abstract: Most production ERC-4337 Paymasters rely on Process-Oriented Abstraction (POA): a centralized off-chain server signs each sponsorship request and therefore acts as a potential censorship bottleneck. We propose Asset-Oriented Abstraction (AOA), which encapsulates payment capability in a persistent, user-owned on-chain asset -- the ``Gas Card'' -- rather than in an off-chain signing process. Following Design Science Research, we implement SuperPaymaster on Optimism Mainnet. Its sponsorship validity is anchored in on-chain Soulbound Token state and deterministic policy rules, removing the off-chain signer as a hard validity gate. We evaluate gas cost on Optimism Mainnet using single-UserOp ERC-20 transfers ($n{=}50$ per system). Trace-level decomposition isolates an approximately 32k-gas delta as the execution cost of eliminating centralized signing.
